Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any materials module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 622. The projective resolution has length at most 317 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to materials structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 622/317 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 211.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Proof: The structural decomposition estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 32 + O(1/n^172). Summing over all modes and applying the materials structural decomposition criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 211.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: Under the optimality condition assumption on materials structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 208. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 375 with relations in degree 1.
